## Agents

### create

`agents.create`

Create Agent. (POST /agents)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.create({ agent_id: 'example', name: 'example', security_domain: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string` | Yes | The developer-assigned Agent identifier, unique within the project. |
| `deployment_id` | `string` | No | The customer deployment this Agent represents. |
| `description` | `string` | No | A human-readable description of the Agent. |
| `memory_settings` | `object` | No | — |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any>` | No | Developer metadata used to organize and filter Agents. |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The Agent's display name. |
| `security_domain` | `string` | Yes | The immutable boundary within which Skills may be synthesized. 1 to 200 characters of lowercase letters, digits, and the characters : . - _ |
| `version` | `string` | No | The customer deployment version this Agent represents. If you omit it, the version is "default". |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="memory_settings full type">

```ts
{
  admission_evaluation_requirements?: {
    requirements: {
      controlled?: boolean,
      environment?: string,
      evaluated_by: string,
      evaluator_version: string,
      result: string
    }[],
    task_family: string
  }[],
  approval?: manual | auto,
  approval_minimum_attempt_families?: number,
  approval_required_tools?: string[],
  auto_approval_task_families?: string[],
  compilation_mode?: scheduled | manual | on_close,
  provisional_expiry_days?: number,
  provisional_skill_limit?: number,
  retention_days?: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_id` | `string \| null` | No | The developer-assigned Agent identifier, unique within the project. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the Agent was created. |
| `deployment_id` | `string \| null` | No | The customer deployment this Agent represents. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | A human-readable description of the Agent. |
| `memory_settings` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Developer metadata used to organize and filter Agents. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The Agent's display name. |
| `revision` | `number \| null` | No | The revision required by the next update or delete request. |
| `security_domain` | `string \| null` | No | The immutable boundary within which Skills may be synthesized. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the Agent was last updated. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The stable identifier of the Agent. |
| `version` | `string \| null` | No | The customer deployment version this Agent represents. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="memory_settings full type">

```ts
{
  admission_evaluation_requirements?: {
    requirements?: {
      controlled?: boolean | null,
      environment?: string | null,
      evaluated_by?: string | null,
      evaluator_version?: string | null,
      result?: string | null
    }[] | null,

  }[] | null,
```

### declareBreakingChange

`agents.declareBreakingChange`

Declare a breaking Agent deployment change. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.declareBreakingChange({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', expected_revision: 1, version: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `expected_revision` | `number` | Yes | The developer supplies the current Agent revision for optimistic concurrency. It must be at least 1. |
| `version` | `string` | Yes | The developer supplies the new deployment version. It must differ from the current version. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `affected_skill_count` | `number \| null` | No | The service returns the number of Skills affected by the declaration. |
| `agent_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the UUID of the Agent that received this declaration. |
| `revision` | `number \| null` | No | The service returns the Agent revision produced by this declaration, stable on replay. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service assigns the UUID that identifies this declaration. |
| `version` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the deployment version recorded by this declaration. |

### delete

`agents.delete`

Delete Agent. Cannot be undone. (DELETE /agents/&#123;agent_uuid&#125;)

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.delete({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', expected_revision: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `expected_revision` | `number` | Yes | The current Agent revision used for optimistic concurrency. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `task` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>

### getContext

`agents.getContext`

Get Agent context. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.getContext({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `environments` | `object[]` | No | The developer supplies environment names used by the Skill. At most 32. |
| `kind` | `string` | No | The developer supplies a Skill kind. At most 64. |
| `max_characters` | `number` | No | The developer sets the maximum number of characters in the context response. |
| `models` | `object[]` | No | The developer supplies models used for this evaluation. At most 32. |
| `objective` | `string` | No | The developer supplies the task-instance objective. At most 512. |
| `task_family` | `string` | No | The developer supplies the task-family slug. At most 128. |
| `tools` | `object[]` | No | The developer supplies tool names used by the Skill. At most 32. |
| `trace_uuid` | `string` | No | The developer supplies the UUID of the source Trace. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="environments full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  version?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="models full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  version?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="tools full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  version?: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `context` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns context for this result. |
| `objective` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the task-instance objective. |
| `partial_sources` | `object[] \| null` | No | The service returns sources that it could not include in full. |
| `request_id` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the UUID that identifies this context request. |
| `skipped` | `object[] \| null` | No | The service returns the Skills that it skipped. |
| `snapshot` | `object[] \| null` | No | The service returns a snapshot of the source data. |
| `task_family` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the task-family slug. |
| `trace_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the UUID of the source Trace. |
| `truncated` | `boolean \| null` | No | The service sets this to true when the context exceeds its output limit. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="partial_sources full type">

```ts
{
  reason?: string | null,
  source?: string | null,
  task_uuid?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="skipped full type">

```ts
{
  reason?: string | null,
  skill_uuid?: string | null,
  version?: number | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="snapshot full type">

```ts
{
  artifact_sha256?: string | null,
  evidence_citations?: string[] | null,
  evidence_counts?: {
    contradicting?: number | null,
    neutral?: number | null,
    supporting?: number | null
  } | null,
  rank?: number | null,
  skill_uuid?: string | null,
  version?: number | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### learning.get

`agents.learning.get`

Get Agent Learning State. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.learning.get({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', task_family: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `task_family` | `string` | Yes | Task family |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `approval` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the approval mode. Valid values are auto and manual. The default is auto. |
| `compilation_mode` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the Agent's learning mode. Valid values are scheduled, manual, and on_close. The default is scheduled. |
| `compilation_pending` | `boolean \| null` | No | The service sets this to true while a learning run waits to start or runs. Skills from that run are not yet available. |
| `eligible_trace_count` | `number \| null` | No | The service reports the number of Traces that can support learning. |
| `minimum_attempt_families` | `number \| null` | No | The service reports how many distinct attempt families a run needs. The default is 3 in scheduled mode and 1 in on_close mode. |
| `next_step` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns text that tells a person what to do next. Code must not treat this text as a stable value. |
| `required_evaluations` | `string[] \| null` | No | The service returns the evaluations that each candidate must pass. |
| `task_family` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the category whose learning progress this record describes. |



---

### learning.listRuns

`agents.learning.listRuns`

List Agent Skill Compilation Outcomes. List durable learning-run outcomes for an Agent. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.learning.listRuns({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `task_family` | `string` | No | Task family |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `total_size` | `number \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  agent_uuid?: string | null,
  attempts?: number | null,
  candidate_count?: number | null,
  compilation_identity?: string | null,
  compilation_window_uuid?: string | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  decision?: string | null,
  proposal_count?: number | null,
  reason_text?: string | null,
  rejected_add_count?: number | null,
  rejection_class?: string | null,
  stage?: string | null,
  task_family?: string | null,
  trace_count?: number | null,
  uuid?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### list

`agents.list`

List Agents. (POST /agents/list)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `order_by` | `created_at \| updated_at \| name` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `agent_id` | `string` | No | Filters to the exact developer-assigned Agent identifier. |
| `deployment_id` | `string` | No | The developer supplies filters to the exact deployment identifier. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any>` | No | Filters to Agents whose metadata contains these key-value pairs. |
| `search` | `string` | No | The developer supplies filters to Agents whose name, Agent identifier, deployment identifier, or deployment version contains this text. Matching is case-insensitive. |
| `status` | `active \| deleting \| deletion_failed` | No | — |
| `version` | `string` | No | Filters to the exact deployment version. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The service returns the Agents on this page. |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | Opaque cursor for retrieving the next page, present only when more results are available. |
| `total_size` | `number \| null` | No | The total number of Agents matching the filters and authorization scope. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  agent_id?: string | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  deployment_id?: string | null,
  description?: string | null,
  memory_settings?: {
    admission_evaluation_requirements?: {
      requirements?: {
        controlled?: boolean | null,
        environment?: string | null,
        evaluated_by?: string | null,
        evaluator_version?: string | null,
        result?: string | null
      }[] | null,
      task_family?: string | null
    }[] | null,
    approval?: string | null,
    approval_minimum_attempt_families?: number | null,
    approval_required_tools?: string[] | null,
    auto_approval_task_families?: string[] | null,
    compilation_mode?: string | null,
    provisional_expiry_days?: number | null,
    provisional_skill_limit?: number | null,
    retention_days?: number | null
  } | null,
  metadata?: Record<string, any> | null,
  name?: string | null,
  revision?: number | null,
  security_domain?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null,
  uuid?: string | null,
  version?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### literalPolicy.get

`agents.literalPolicy.get`

Get Agent Literal Policy. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.literalPolicy.get({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `allowlisted_classes` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `allowlisted_values` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time this explicit revision was created. Omitted for revision zero. |
| `created_by_principal_id` | `string \| null` | No | The principal identifier that created this explicit revision. Empty for revision zero. |
| `created_by_principal_type` | `string \| null` | No | The principal type that created this explicit revision. Empty for revision zero. |
| `default` | `string \| null` | No | The remediation for literals that are not explicitly allowed. |
| `digest` | `string \| null` | No | SHA-256 of the canonical policy JSON. |
| `revision` | `number \| null` | No | The immutable policy revision. Revision zero is the built-in strict default. |

### literalPolicy.update

`agents.literalPolicy.update`

Update Agent Literal Policy. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.literalPolicy.update({
	agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	allowlisted_classes: {
		environments: ['example'],
		tools: ['example'],
	},
	allowlisted_values: {
		environments: ['example'],
		tools: ['example'],
	},
	default: 'parameterize',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `allowlisted_classes` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `allowlisted_values` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `default` | `parameterize \| remove \| reject` | Yes | The remediation for literals that are not explicitly allowed. |
| `expected_revision` | `number` | No | The current literal-policy revision used for optimistic concurrency. |

### planSplit

`agents.planSplit`

Plan an Agent split. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.planSplit({
	agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	destinations: [{
		agent: { agent_id: 'example', name: 'example', security_domain: 'example' },
		skills: [{ skill_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', skill_version_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', version: 1 }],
	}],
	expected_revision: 1,
	rationale: 'example',
	review_acknowledged: true,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Source Agent UUID |
| `destinations` | `object[]` | Yes | The developer lists 1 to 100 destination Agents. |
| `expected_revision` | `number` | Yes | The developer supplies the current revision used for optimistic concurrency. At least 1. |
| `rationale` | `string` | Yes | The developer explains the requested change. The limit is 2,000 characters. |
| `review_acknowledged` | `boolean` | Yes | The developer sets this to true to confirm that the review is complete. |

### skillCandidates.evaluate

`agents.skillCandidates.evaluate`

Record Agent Skill Candidate Evaluation.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.skillCandidates.evaluate({
	agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	review_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	candidate_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	evaluated_by: 'customer',
	evaluator_version: 'example',
	expected_revision: 1,
	verdict: 'succeeded',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `review_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Candidate review UUID |
| `candidate_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Candidate UUID |
| `controlled_test` | `boolean` | No | The developer sets this to true when a controlled test produced the result. |
| `environment` | `string` | No | The developer supplies the environment for this evaluation. |
| `evaluated_by` | `customer \| tool \| offline_corpus \| policy` | Yes | The developer selects the evaluation source. Valid values are customer, tool, offline_corpus, policy. |
| `evaluator_version` | `string` | Yes | The developer supplies the version of the evaluation rule. |
| `evidence_reference` | `string` | No | The developer links this evaluation to an evidence record. |
| `expected_revision` | `number` | Yes | The developer supplies the current revision used for optimistic concurrency. At least 1. |
| `metrics` | `Record<string, number>` | No | The developer supplies evaluation metrics. |
| `verdict` | `succeeded \| failed \| partial \| unknown` | Yes | The developer supplies evaluation verdict. Valid values are succeeded, failed, partial, unknown. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `candidate_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the UUID of the candidate that this evaluation covers. |
| `controlled_test` | `boolean \| null` | No | The service reports whether a controlled test produced the result. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns when Zep created this resource. |
| `environment` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the environment used for this evaluation. |
| `evaluated_by` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the evaluation source. Valid values are customer, tool, offline_corpus, and policy. |
| `evaluator_version` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the version of the evaluation rule. |
| `evidence_reference` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the link to supporting evidence, when one exists. |
| `metrics` | `Record<string, number> \| null` | No | The service returns evaluation metrics. |
| `review_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the UUID of the review that produced this evaluation. |
| `revision` | `number \| null` | No | The service returns the current revision number. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the UUID that identifies this resource. |
| `verdict` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the evaluation verdict. |

### skillPublications.lookup

`agents.skillPublications.lookup`

Look up automatic Skill publication lineage. Find a destination Skill by its source Agent, Skill, and version. The endpoint returns 404 until an automatic publication is ready or after it is invalidated. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.skillPublications.lookup({
	agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	source_agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	source_skill_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	source_version: 1,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Destination Agent UUID |
| `source_agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Source Agent UUID |
| `source_skill_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Source Skill UUID |
| `source_version` | `number` | Yes | Source version number |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `destination_agent_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The destination Agent UUID. |
| `destination_skill_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The destination Skill UUID. |
| `destination_version_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The destination version UUID. |
| `policy_revision` | `number \| null` | No | The policy revision that allowed automatic publication. |
| `policy_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The policy UUID that allowed automatic publication. |
| `publication_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The publication UUID. |
| `source_agent_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The source Agent UUID. |
| `source_skill_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The source Skill UUID. |
| `source_version` | `number \| null` | No | The source version number. |
| `source_version_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The source version UUID. |

| `type` | `string \| null` | No | The kind of asynchronous operation the task represents. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the task was last updated. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the task, used to poll its status. |

### skills.versions.exports.get

`agents.skills.versions.exports.get`

Download an Agent Skill export. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.skills.versions.exports.get({
	agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	skill_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	version: 1,
	task_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `skill_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Skill UUID, or the 8-character short_id |
| `version` | `number` | Yes | Skill version |
| `task_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Export Task UUID |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### skills.versions.restore

`agents.skills.versions.restore`

Restore Agent Skill Version. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.skills.versions.restore({
	agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	skill_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	expected_version: 1,
	version: 1,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `skill_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Skill UUID, or the 8-character short_id |
| `expected_version` | `number` | Yes | The developer supplies the current Skill version to protect against concurrent edits. |
| `version` | `number` | Yes | The developer supplies the Skill version to restore. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contract` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns when Zep created this resource. |
| `definition` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `markdown` | `string \| null` | No | Markdown is the agent view of the Skill unless the request selects `markdown_format=full`. |
| `self_contained` | `boolean \| null` | No | The service reports whether the Skill can run without external context. |
| `version` | `number \| null` | No | The service returns the Skill version number. |

### taskFamilies.list

`agents.taskFamilies.list`

List Agent Task Families. (POST /agents/&#123;agent_uuid&#125;/task-families/list)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.taskFamilies.list({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The discovered task families on this page. |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | Opaque cursor for retrieving the next page, present only when more results are available. |
| `total_size` | `number \| null` | No | The service returns the total number of discovered task families within the owning Agent. |

### traceImports.create

`agents.traceImports.create`

Create a trace import. Create a scheduled import or queue a one-time import. A scheduled import returns HTTP 201 with import. A one-time import returns HTTP 202 with import, run, and task. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.traceImports.create({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `connection_uuid` | `string` | No | The developer selects the configured provider connection. |
| `learn_from` | `boolean` | No | LearnFrom controls whether imported evidence can support Skills. |
| `mapping` | `object` | No | — |
| `name` | `string` | No | Name is the import display name. |
| `on_source_change` | `ignore \| replace` | No | — |
| `provider_project_id` | `string` | No | The developer selects the project to import from the provider. |
| `require_review` | `boolean` | No | RequireReview requires review for candidates backed by this import. |
| `schedule` | `object` | No | — |
| `selection` | `object` | No | — |

```ts
}
```

### traceImports.delete

`agents.traceImports.delete`

Delete a trace import. Set traces=delete to queue asynchronous Trace deletion. The default keeps Traces. Example query: ?traces=delete. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.traceImports.delete({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', import_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `import_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Trace import UUID |
| `traces` | `keep \| delete` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `completed_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the task reached a terminal status; absent while it is still in progress. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the task was created. |
| `error` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `progress` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `result` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `started_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time a worker began processing the task; absent until then. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the task state. Valid values are pending, processing, succeeded, partial, and failed. A task has no canceled state. When a batch becomes canceled, its batch.process task becomes failed, and the task error code is batch_canceled. |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | The kind of asynchronous operation the task represents. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the task was last updated. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the task, used to poll its status. |

`agents.traceImports.runs.create`

Create a trace import run. Create a pending run and its Task. This operation does not start the run. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `write`


|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `completed_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the task reached a terminal status; absent while it is still in progress. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the task was created. |
| `error` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `progress` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `result` | `object \| null` | No | — |

| `type` | `string \| null` | No | The kind of asynchronous operation the task represents. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the task was last updated. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the task, used to poll its status. |

| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | Items contains runs on this page. Example: [&#123;"uuid":"ea7e2724-9bfd-4ca1-aa5c-40a1dd72a688","status":"pending"&#125;]. |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | NextCursor is the cursor for the next page. |
| `total_size` | `number \| null` | No | TotalSize is the number of matching runs. |

### traceImports.update

`agents.traceImports.update`

Update a trace import. Use expected_revision to reject a stale update. Example request: &#123;"expected_revision":1,"name":"Updated support traces"&#125;. Agent Skills is in closed release: accounts without access get 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.traceImports.update({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', import_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `import_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Trace import UUID |
| `expected_revision` | `number` | No | ExpectedRevision is the current import revision. |
| `learn_from` | `boolean` | No | LearnFrom is the new learning setting, when supplied. |
| `mapping` | `object` | No | — |
| `name` | `string` | No | Name is the new import name, when supplied. |
| `on_source_change` | `ignore \| replace` | No | OnSourceChange is the new change policy, when supplied. |
| `require_review` | `boolean` | No | RequireReview is the new review setting, when supplied. |
| `schedule` | `object` | No | — |
| `selection` | `object` | No | — |

### traces.appendEvent

`agents.traces.appendEvent`

Append Agent Trace Event. A tool call and its result can share a tool_call_id. For example, append &#123;"event_type":"tool_call","tool_call_id":"call_8f2b","content":"Search orders","context":&#123;"tools":[&#123;"name":"search_orders"&#125;]&#125;&#125; and &#123;"event_type":"tool_result","tool_call_id":"call_8f2b","content":"Order…

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.traces.appendEvent({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', trace_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', event_type: 'input_reference' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `trace_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Trace UUID |
| `content` | `string` | No | — |
| `context` | `object` | No | — |
| `event_id` | `string` | No | — |
| `event_type` | `input_reference \| observation \| decision \| tool_call \| tool_result \| artifact \| checkpoint \| feedback \| correction \| reflection \| verification` | Yes | — |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any>` | No | Metadata is developer data. Skills learning does not read it. |
| `occurred_at` | `string` | No | — |
| `sequence` | `number` | No | — |
| `sources` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `tool_call_id` | `string` | No | ToolCallID is the ID that the agent framework gives a tool call. Use the same ID on the tool_call and tool_result events. It is valid only on these event types. A result without a matching call is valid. OpenAI uses tool_calls[].id, Anthropic uses the tool_use block id, and LangChain uses ToolMessage.tool_call_id. |

### traces.create

`agents.traces.create`

Create Agent Trace. (POST /agents/&#123;agent_uuid&#125;/traces)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.traces.create({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', objective: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `learn_from` | `boolean` | No | The developer supplies whether eligible evidence may participate in learning. Defaults to true. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any>` | No | Developer metadata used to organize and filter Traces. |
| `objective` | `string` | Yes | The developer describes the task instance. The create request requires a non-empty value but does not check its size. An AppendAgentTraceEventRequest returns 400 if the Trace objective exceeds 8 KiB (8,192 bytes). |
| `parent_trace_uuid` | `string` | No | The developer supplies the parent Trace UUID when this attempt retries an earlier attempt. |
| `task_family` | `string` | No | The developer may set a task-family slug. If the developer omits it, Agent Skills assigns the Trace to a task family automatically. If Agent Skills cannot assign a task family, the `task_family_inference` field of the Trace is `unassigned`. A BYOM configuration requires `task_family`. A strict-residency deployment requires `task_family`. Without task_family in either case, the API returns 400 `invalid_request` with the message `task_family is required`. The slug must contain 1 to 128 lowercase letters, digits, `.`, `_`, `/`, or `-`. It must start with a lowercase letter or digit. |
| `trace_id` | `string` | No | The developer may set an identifier that is unique within the Agent. A retry with the same create parameters returns 200 and the existing Trace. Different parameters return 409. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `abandoned_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the Trace was abandoned. |
| `agent_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the owning Agent UUID. |
| `attempt_family_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the stable retry-family identity shared by attempts. |
| `closed_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the Trace closed. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the Trace was created. |
| `internal` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `learn_from` | `boolean \| null` | No | The service returns whether eligible evidence may participate in learning. |
| `learn_from_reason` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns why the Trace is not eligible for learning, when it is not eligible. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Developer metadata used for exact containment filters. |
| `objective` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the task-instance objective captured for this attempt. |
| `outcome` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `parent_trace_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the parent attempt when this Trace is a retry. |
| `revision` | `number \| null` | No | The service returns the current revision for the next update or lifecycle command. |
| `source` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `task_family` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the declared or assigned task-family slug, when one is available. |
| `task_family_inference` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `task_family_source` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns how it selected the task family. Valid values are declared, import_rule, and inferred. |
| `trace_id` | `string \| null` | No | The developer may set an identifier that is unique within the Agent. A retry with the same create parameters returns 200 and the existing Trace. Different parameters return 409. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the Trace was last updated. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the stable Trace UUID. |
| `verification` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### traces.delete

`agents.traces.delete`

Delete Agent Trace. Cannot be undone. (DELETE /agents/&#123;agent_uuid&#125;/traces/&#123;trace_uuid&#125;)

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.traces.delete({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', trace_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', expected_revision: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `trace_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Trace UUID |
| `expected_revision` | `number` | Yes | The developer supplies the current revision for optimistic concurrency. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `affected_skills` | `number \| null` | No | The service returns the number of Skills affected by this deletion. |
| `deletion_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the UUID of the deletion operation. |
| `skill_repair_task` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `summary_repair_task` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `trace` | `object \| null` | No | — |

### traces.list

`agents.traces.list`

List Agent Traces. (POST /agents/&#123;agent_uuid&#125;/traces/list)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.traces.list({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `order_by` | `created_at \| updated_at` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `import_uuid` | `string` | No | Filter by owning Trace import UUID |
| `source_kind` | `native \| trace_import` | No | — |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any>` | No | Filters to Traces whose metadata contains these key-value pairs. |
| `outcome` | `succeeded \| failed \| partial \| abandoned \| unknown` | No | — |
| `parent_trace_uuid` | `string` | No | Filters to direct retries of this parent Trace. |
| `status` | `open \| closing \| closed \| abandoned \| deleted` | No | — |
| `task_family` | `string` | No | Filters to an exact task-family slug. |
| `verification` | `none \| agent \| tool \| customer \| external` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The Traces on this page. |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | Opaque cursor for retrieving the next page, present only when more results are available. |
| `total_size` | `number \| null` | No | The total number of Traces matching the filters within the owning Agent. |

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.agents.traces.reopen({ agent_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', trace_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', expected_revision: 1 });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

| `agent_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Agent UUID |
| `trace_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Trace UUID |
| `expected_revision` | `number` | Yes | The current Trace revision used for optimistic concurrency. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

| `learn_from_reason` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns why the Trace is not eligible for learning, when it is not eligible. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Developer metadata used for exact containment filters. |
| `objective` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the task-instance objective captured for this attempt. |
| `outcome` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `parent_trace_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the parent attempt when this Trace is a retry. |
| `revision` | `number \| null` | No | The service returns the current revision for the next update or lifecycle command. |
| `source` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `task_family` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the declared or assigned task-family slug, when one is available. |
| `task_family_inference` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `task_family_source` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns how it selected the task family. Valid values are declared, import_rule, and inferred. |
| `trace_id` | `string \| null` | No | The developer may set an identifier that is unique within the Agent. A retry with the same create parameters returns 200 and the existing Trace. Different parameters return 409. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the Trace was last updated. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the stable Trace UUID. |
| `verification` | `string \| null` | No | — |

`batches.get`

Get batch. (GET /batches/&#123;batch_uuid&#125;)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.batches.get({ batch_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `batch_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Batch UUID |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the batch was created. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Custom metadata associated with the batch, supplied when it was created. |
| `progress` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | The processing status of the batch: draft, invalid, queued, processing, succeeded, partial, failed, or canceled. |
| `strict_ontology` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether ontology enforcement is applied when this batch is processed, rejecting entities that do not match the configured ontology. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the batch. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="progress full type">

```ts
{
  failed_items?: number | null,
  processing_items?: number | null,
  stage?: string | null,
  succeeded_items?: number | null,
  total_items?: number | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### list

`batches.list`

List batches. (GET /batches)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.batches.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `status` | `draft \| invalid \| queued \| processing \| succeeded \| partial \| failed \| canceled` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The batches on this page. |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | Opaque cursor for retrieving the next page, present only when more results are available. |
| `total_size` | `number \| null` | No | The total number of batches that match the request. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  created_at?: string | null,
  metadata?: Record<string, any> | null,
  progress?: {
    failed_items?: number | null,
    processing_items?: number | null,
    stage?: string | null,
    succeeded_items?: number | null,
    total_items?: number | null
  } | null,
  status?: string | null,
  strict_ontology?: boolean | null,
  uuid?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Debug Logs

### enable

`debugLogs.enable`

Enable debug logging. Enables debug logging for the project for one hour, or for 24 hours on the Enterprise plan. Episodes ingested while debug logging is enabled have a debug log (see graph.episode.get_debug_logs).

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.debugLogs.enable({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `duration_seconds` | `number \| null` | No | The number of seconds that debug logging stays enabled. |
| `ingestion_trace_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Tells if ingestion tracing was also enabled. Ingestion tracing is available on the Flex, Flex Plus, and Enterprise plans. |

### documentSummaries.list

`graphs.documentSummaries.list`

List document summaries. Lists the document summaries of a graph and returns one page of results. The default page_size is 50, and the maximum page_size is 100. You can sort by uuid or created_at. The default sort is uuid in descending order.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.documentSummaries.list({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `order_by` | `uuid \| created_at` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `filters` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="filters full type">

```ts
{
  date_filters?: {
    any_of?: {
      all_of?: {
        field?: created_at | valid_at | invalid_at | expired_at,
        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | is_null | is_not_null,
        value?: string
      }[]
    }[]
  },
  metadata_filters?: {
    filters?: {
      operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
      property_name?: string,
      value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
    }[],
    groups?: {
      filters?: {
        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
        property_name?: string,
        value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
      }[],
      groups?: (recursive)[],
      type?: and | or
    }[],
    type?: and | or
  },
  property_filters?: {
    operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | is_null | is_not_null,
    property_name?: string,
    value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
  }[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The document summaries on this page. |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | Opaque cursor for retrieving the next page, present only when more results are available. |
| `total_size` | `number \| null` | No | Omitted for this collection; use next_cursor to detect the end of pagination. |

<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
  created_at?: string | null,

  last_summarized_at?: string | null,
  last_summarized_episode_valid_at?: string | null,
  summary?: string | null,
  uuid?: string | null
}[] | null
```

### edges.add

`graphs.edges.add`

Add edges. Adds 1 to 100 edges. A name creates a node when deduplicate is false. When deduplicate is true, Zep matches a node by name first.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.edges.add({
	graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	edges: [{
		fact: 'example',
		fact_name: 'example',
		source_node: {},
		target_node: {},
	}],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `deduplicate` | `boolean` | No | When true, Zep compares each edge with graph edges and can merge a duplicate or invalidate a contradicted edge. This adds an LLM call per edge. The default is false. |
| `edges` | `object[]` | Yes | The edges to add to the graph. The request accepts 1 to 100 edges. |

<AccordionGroup>

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.edges.get({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', edge_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `attributes` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Additional attributes of the edge. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the edge was created. |
| `episode_uuids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The episodes this edge was derived from. |
| `expired_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the fact was superseded or removed. |
| `fact` | `string \| null` | No | The natural-language fact this edge represents. |
| `graph_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the graph this edge belongs to. |
| `hyperedge_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the hyperedge this edge is a member of. Omitted when the edge is not part of a hyperedge. |
| `invalid_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time at which the fact stopped being true. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the edge, in upper snake case, for example RELATES_TO. |
| `relevance` | `number \| null` | No | A cross-encoder-derived relevance score, present only when the cross-encoder reranker scored this result; like score, it is omitted from plain list results. |
| `scope` | `string \| null` | No | The kind of edge this is, for example entity or episodic. |
| `score` | `number \| null` | No | The ranking score returned with every search result; omitted when the edge appears in a plain list rather than a search result. |
| `source_node_labels` | `string[] \| null` | No | The current labels of the source node. This is read at request time rather than stored on the edge, and is omitted when the source node cannot be resolved. |
| `source_node_name` | `string \| null` | No | The current name of the source node. This is read at request time rather than stored on the edge, and is omitted when the source node cannot be resolved. |
| `source_node_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the edge's source node. |
| `target_node_labels` | `string[] \| null` | No | The current labels of the target node. This is read at request time rather than stored on the edge, and is omitted when the target node cannot be resolved. |
| `target_node_name` | `string \| null` | No | The current name of the target node. This is read at request time rather than stored on the edge, and is omitted when the target node cannot be resolved. |
| `target_node_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the edge's target node. |
| `user_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the user that owns the graph. Present only when the graph is a user graph. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the edge. |
| `valid_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time from which the fact is considered true. |

### edges.list

`graphs.edges.list`

List edges. Lists the edges of a graph and returns one page of results. The default page_size is 50, and the maximum page_size is 100. You can sort by uuid, created_at, or valid_at. The default sort is uuid in descending order. An edge with no valid_at sorts as if its valid_at is 1970-01-01T00:00:00Z.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.edges.list({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `order_by` | `uuid \| created_at \| valid_at` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `filters` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="filters full type">

```ts
{
  connected_node_uuids?: string[],
  content_policy_violated?: boolean,
  date_filters?: {
    any_of?: {
      all_of?: {
        field?: created_at | valid_at | invalid_at | expired_at,
        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | is_null | is_not_null,
        value?: string
      }[]
    }[]
  },
  edge_types?: string[],
  edge_uuids?: string[],
  episode_uuids?: string[],
  exclude_edge_types?: string[],
  exclude_node_labels?: string[],
  metadata_filters?: {
    filters?: {
      operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
      property_name?: string,
      value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
    }[],
    groups?: {
      filters?: {
        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
        property_name?: string,
        value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
      }[],
      groups?: (recursive)[],
      type?: and | or
    }[],
    type?: and | or
  },
  node_labels?: string[],
  property_filters?: {
    operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | is_null | is_not_null,
    property_name?: string,
    value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
  }[],
  source_node_uuids?: string[],
  target_node_uuids?: string[]
}
```

### edges.update

`graphs.edges.update`

Update edge. Updates one edge. When the edge belongs to a hyperedge, changing fact rewrites it on every member of that hyperedge in one all-or-nothing write, because the members share it. Attribute-only edits touch this edge alone.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.edges.update({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', edge_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `edge_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Edge UUID |
| `attributes` | `object \| null` | No | Additional attributes to merge onto the edge; a key set to null is removed. |
| `fact` | `string` | No | The fact text describing the relationship between the source and target nodes. |

### episodes.add

`graphs.episodes.add`

Add episode. (POST /graphs/&#123;graph_uuid&#125;/episodes)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.episodes.add({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', data: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `data` | `string` | Yes | The episode content to add to the graph. |
| `document_id` | `string` | No | Groups this episode as a chunk of a document on the graph. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any>` | No | Metadata to store on the episode. Max 10 keys. Values must be strings, numbers, booleans, or arrays of scalars. |
| `reference_time` | `string` | No | The episode's reference time, used for temporal reasoning rather than ingestion time. Defaults to the ingestion time. |
| `source_description` | `string` | No | A description of the source of this episode. |
| `strict_ontology` | `boolean` | No | When true, prevents extraction of generic entity nodes that do not match the configured ontology. |
| `type` | `text \| json \| message` | No | The data format of the episode: text, json, or message. Defaults to text. |

| `document_id` | `string \| null` | No | The developer-assigned identifier of the document this episode belongs to, present only for episodes added to a document. |
| `expired_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time when Zep stopped the processing of the episode because the episode waited for more than 7 days. An expired episode is not in the graph. Add the episode again to process it. The field is omitted for an episode that did not expire. |
| `graph_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the graph this episode belongs to. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Custom metadata associated with the episode. |
| `processed` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the episode has finished graph extraction and indexing. |
| `reference_time` | `string \| null` | No | The episode's reference time, used for temporal reasoning: the time that the developer supplied on add, or the ingestion time when the developer supplied no time. |
| `relevance` | `number \| null` | No | A cross-encoder-derived relevance score, present only when the cross-encoder reranker scored this result; like score, it is omitted from plain list results. |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `role_name` | `string \| null` | No | The free-form display name of the episode's sender, present only when the episode was added as a message. |
| `score` | `number \| null` | No | The ranking score returned with every search result; omitted when the episode appears in a plain list rather than a search result. |
| `source` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `source_description` | `string \| null` | No | A free-form description of where the episode's content came from. |
| `thread_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The thread this episode belongs to, present only for episodes added to a thread. |
| `user_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the user that owns the graph. Present only when the graph is a user graph. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the episode. |
| `wait_status` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### episodes.getDebugLogs

`graphs.episodes.getDebugLogs`

Get episode debug logs. Returns the ingestion workflow log of an episode. The log exists only when debug logging was enabled for the project when the episode was ingested (see debug_log.enable).

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.episodes.getDebugLogs({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', episode_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `episode_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Episode UUID |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `completed_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the ingestion workflow completed, present only after completion. |
| `entries` | `object[] \| null` | No | The log entries, oldest first. |
| `error` | `string \| null` | No | The error message, present only when the status is error. |
| `started_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the ingestion workflow started. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |

```ts
}[] | null
```

### episodes.list

`graphs.episodes.list`

List episodes. Lists the episodes of a graph. filters.mentioned_node_uuids restricts the results to episodes that mention any of the listed node UUIDs. The list can also contain episode UUIDs: an episode UUID matches that episode, so one request can return a known set of episodes. At most 256 entries.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.episodes.list({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `order_by` | `created_at \| uuid` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `filters` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="filters full type">

```ts
  content_policy_violated?: boolean,
  document_id?: string,
  mentioned_node_uuids?: string[],

      property_name?: string,

}
```

### episodes.listIngestionTraces

`graphs.episodes.listIngestionTraces`

List episode ingestion traces. Returns the ingestion traces of an episode, oldest first. Each trace records the input and the output of one ingestion step, with an explanation on each output entry that has one.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.episodes.listIngestionTraces({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', episode_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `episode_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Episode UUID |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The ingestion traces on this page. |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | The cursor to pass as the next request's cursor to fetch the following page; absent when no further pages remain. |

### getContentPolicy

`graphs.getContentPolicy`

Get graph content policy. Experimental: the request, the response, and the behavior of this operation can change. Returns the content policy the graph bound at creation. The policy of a graph does not change after creation. A graph without a content policy returns revision 0 with no categories and no rules.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.getContentPolicy({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `bound_at` | `string \| null` | No | When the graph bound the policy. |
| `categories` | `object[] \| null` | No | The categories of the policy. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | When this policy set was created. |
| `project_revision` | `number \| null` | No | The project revision number this graph bound at creation. It always equals revision. |
| `revision` | `number \| null` | No | The project revision number. 0 is the empty policy of a new project. |
| `rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | The rules of the policy. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The identifier of this policy set. A project revision and every graph that binds it without additions share one uuid. |

```ts
  description?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  key?: string | null
}[] | null
```

```ts
  id?: string | null,
```

### getInclusionRules

`graphs.getInclusionRules`

Get graph inclusion rules. Returns the graph rules, for example &#123;"inclusion_rules":[],"inherited":true&#125;. A free project answered 404 not_found to both the read and the write (11-27): the feature may need enabling on the account.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.getInclusionRules({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Graph UUID |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `inclusion_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | InclusionRules replaces the complete rule set at this scope. |
| `inherited` | `boolean \| null` | No | Inherited identifies a graph that uses project defaults. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="inclusion_rules full type">

```ts
{
  condition?: string | null,
  effect?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  target?: string | null,
  types?: string[] | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### getKnowledge

`graphs.getKnowledge`

Get graph knowledge. Returns the domain knowledge in effect at this scope. The knowledge_api account flag must be enabled. Response example: &#123;"knowledge":[&#123;"name":"CRM","text":"CRM means the customer service."&#125;],"inherited":false&#125;

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.getKnowledge({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph or its user. |

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `inherited` | `boolean \| null` | No | Inherited is true when the graph uses the project entries. |
| `knowledge` | `object[] \| null` | No | The domain knowledge entries in effect at this scope. |

<Accordion title="knowledge full type">

```ts
  text?: string | null
}[] | null
```

### getSubgraph

`graphs.getSubgraph`

Get subgraph. (POST /graphs/&#123;graph_uuid&#125;/subgraph)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.getSubgraph({
	graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	seed_node_uuids: ['3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `depth` | `number` | No | The maximum traversal depth from the seed nodes. Defaults to 1. |
| `direction` | `in \| out \| both` | No | The edge orientation to follow during expansion: in, out, or both. Defaults to both. |
| `filters` | `object` | No | — |
| `max_edges` | `number` | No | The maximum number of edges in the response. Defaults to 200. |
| `max_nodes` | `number` | No | The maximum number of nodes in the response. Defaults to 100. |
| `seed_node_uuids` | `string[]` | Yes | The node UUIDs to expand from, in traversal-priority order. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="filters full type">

```ts
  connected_node_uuids?: string[],
  content_policy_violated?: boolean,
  date_filters?: {

        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | is_null | is_not_null,
        value?: string
      }[]
    }[]
  },

        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
        property_name?: string,

      }[],
      groups?: (recursive)[],
      type?: and | or
    }[],
    type?: and | or
  },

    property_name?: string,

  }[],
  source_node_uuids?: string[],
  target_node_uuids?: string[]
}
```

### hyperedges.add

`graphs.hyperedges.add`

Add hyperedge. Creates one hyperedge: a fact that relates more than two nodes, written onto one member edge per node pair. The member edges must span at least three distinct nodes, since two nodes are a pair of edges rather than a hyperedge; a single pair uses graph.edge.add.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.hyperedges.add({
	graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	edges: [{
		name: 'example',
		source_node: {},
		target_node: {},
	}],
	fact: 'example',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `attributes` | `Record<string, any>` | No | Additional attributes to store on every member edge. |
| `edges` | `object[]` | Yes | The member edges to create. Each pair becomes one edge carrying the shared fact. |
| `expired_at` | `string` | No | The time at which the fact was superseded or invalidated. |
| `fact` | `string` | Yes | The natural-language fact, written onto every member edge. |
| `invalid_at` | `string` | No | The time at which the fact stopped being true. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any>` | No | Metadata attached to the episode created for this hyperedge. |
| `valid_at` | `string` | No | The time at which the fact became true. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="edges full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  source_node: {
    attributes?: Record<string, any>,
    labels?: string[],
    name?: string,
    summary?: string,
    uuid?: string
  },
  target_node: {
    attributes?: Record<string, any>,
    labels?: string[],
    name?: string,
    summary?: string,
    uuid?: string
  }
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `hyperedge` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `task` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<Accordion title="hyperedge full type">

```ts
  attributes?: Record<string, any> | null,

    source_node?: {

      summary?: string | null,
      uuid?: string | null
    } | null,

      labels?: string[] | null,
      name?: string | null,
      summary?: string | null,
      uuid?: string | null
    } | null,
    uuid?: string | null
  }[] | null,

} | null
```

### hyperedges.createEdge

`graphs.hyperedges.createEdge`

Add hyperedge member. Adds one member edge to an existing hyperedge. The new member inherits the hyperedge's fact and timestamps and joins its episodes, so only its own name and node pair are supplied. The response contains the UUID that Zep assigned and the name and nodes that you supplied.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.hyperedges.createEdge({
	graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	hyperedge_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	name: 'example',
	source_node: {},
	target_node: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `hyperedge_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Hyperedge UUID |
| `name` | `string` | Yes | The name of the new member edge, in upper snake case. |
| `source_node` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `target_node` | `object` | Yes | — |

### hyperedges.delete

`graphs.hyperedges.delete`

Delete hyperedge. Deletes every member edge of the hyperedge. After the task completes the hyperedge and each of its members are gone. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**


### hyperedges.deleteEdge

`graphs.hyperedges.deleteEdge`

Delete hyperedge member. Removes one member edge from the hyperedge. The remaining members stay in the hyperedge, and deleting the last member removes the hyperedge itself. Cannot be undone.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.hyperedges.deleteEdge({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', hyperedge_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', edge_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

| `edge_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Edge UUID |
