### hyperedges.get

`graphs.hyperedges.get`

Get hyperedge. Returns one hyperedge assembled from its member edges. The fact and the validity timestamps are shared by every member and are reported on the hyperedge; each member reports only its own name and endpoints.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.hyperedges.get({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', hyperedge_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `hyperedge_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Hyperedge UUID |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the hyperedge was created. Every member edge shares this value. |
| `edges` | `object[] \| null` | No | The member edges, ordered by uuid. Each carries only its own identity and endpoints; the shared fact and timestamps are on the hyperedge. |
| `expired_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the fact was superseded or removed. |
| `fact` | `string \| null` | No | The natural-language fact this hyperedge represents. Every member edge carries the same fact. |
| `graph_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the graph this hyperedge belongs to. |
| `invalid_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time at which the fact stopped being true. |
| `user_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the user that owns the graph. Present only when the graph is a user graph. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the hyperedge. |
| `valid_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time from which the fact is considered true. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="edges full type">

```ts
{
  name?: string | null,
  source_node_uuid?: string | null,
  target_node_uuid?: string | null,
  uuid?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### hyperedges.list

`graphs.hyperedges.list`

List hyperedges. Returns the graph's hyperedges. A hyperedge is listed while it has at least one member edge. Supported filters are node_uuids, edge_uuids and episode_uuids: a hyperedge matches a list when any of its members does, and must match every list supplied.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.hyperedges.list({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `order_by` | `uuid \| created_at` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `filters` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="filters full type">

```ts
{
  edge_uuids?: string[],
  episode_uuids?: string[],
  node_uuids?: string[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  created_at?: string | null,
  edges?: {
    name?: string | null,
    source_node_uuid?: string | null,
    target_node_uuid?: string | null,
    uuid?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  expired_at?: string | null,
  fact?: string | null,
  graph_uuid?: string | null,
  invalid_at?: string | null,
  user_uuid?: string | null,
  uuid?: string | null,
  valid_at?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### hyperedges.update

`graphs.hyperedges.update`

Update hyperedge. Updates the shared fact and writes it onto every member edge in one all-or-nothing write: either every member carries the new fact or none does. Only fact is accepted, because it is the only field the members share.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.hyperedges.update({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', hyperedge_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `hyperedge_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Hyperedge UUID |
| `fact` | `string` | No | The natural-language fact, rewritten onto every member edge. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the hyperedge was created. Every member edge shares this value. |
| `edges` | `object[] \| null` | No | The member edges, ordered by uuid. Each carries only its own identity and endpoints; the shared fact and timestamps are on the hyperedge. |
| `expired_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the fact was superseded or removed. |
| `fact` | `string \| null` | No | The natural-language fact this hyperedge represents. Every member edge carries the same fact. |
| `graph_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the graph this hyperedge belongs to. |
| `invalid_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time at which the fact stopped being true. |
| `user_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the user that owns the graph. Present only when the graph is a user graph. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the hyperedge. |
| `valid_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time from which the fact is considered true. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="edges full type">

```ts
{
  name?: string | null,
  source_node_uuid?: string | null,
  target_node_uuid?: string | null,
  uuid?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### listContentPolicyEvents

`graphs.listContentPolicyEvents`

List graph content policy events. Experimental: the request, the response, and the behavior of this operation can change. Lists the content policy decisions recorded for a graph, newest first. Each event carries identifiers only. A graph without a content policy returns an empty list.

**Risk:** `read`

### lookup


| `user_id` | `string` | No | The developer-assigned user ID to resolve to a UUID. Mutually exclusive with thread_id and graph_id. |

| `description` | `string \| null` | No | A description of the graph. |
| `graph_id` | `string \| null` | No | The developer-assigned identifier of the graph, present only for standard graphs. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The display name of the graph. |
| `time_zone` | `string \| null` | No | The graph's IANA time zone. |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | The kind of graph: user for a user's personal graph, or standard for a named graph created directly. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the graph was last updated. |
| `user_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the user this graph belongs to, present only for a user graph. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the graph. |

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.nodes.get({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', node_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `node_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Node UUID |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `attributes` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Additional attributes of the node, as defined by its entity type. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the node was created. |
| `degree` | `number \| null` | No | The count of live entity edges that touch this node, in both directions. Present only when the list request orders by `degree`. |
| `episode_uuids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The UUIDs of the live episodes that mention this node, newest first. The list is complete when `episode_uuids_truncated` is false. The list holds the newest 100 when the node has more than 100 source episodes; list episodes with the `mentioned_node_uuids` filter to read them all. |
| `episode_uuids_truncated` | `boolean \| null` | No | True when the node has more than 100 source episodes, so `episode_uuids` holds only the newest 100, or when provenance is unavailable. False means `episode_uuids` is the complete set. |
| `graph_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the graph this node belongs to. |
| `labels` | `string[] \| null` | No | The entity type labels assigned to the node. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the entity that the node represents. |
| `relevance` | `number \| null` | No | A cross-encoder-derived relevance score, present only when the cross-encoder reranker scored this result; like score, it is omitted from plain list results. |
| `score` | `number \| null` | No | The ranking score returned with every search result; omitted when the node appears in a plain list rather than a search result. |
| `summary` | `string \| null` | No | A generated summary of the entity that the node represents. |
| `user_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the user that owns the graph. Present only when the graph is a user graph. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The node UUID, assigned by Zep. |

### query

`graphs.query`

Query a graph. Experimental. Smart Query retrieves multi-hop context from a graph in one call. Some agent tasks need multi-hop retrieval: the agent does not know in advance what to search for, so it must retrieve a record, learn from it, and then plan the next retrieval.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.query({
	graph_uuids: ['3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c'],
	query: 'example',
	stream: true,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `generate_answer` | `boolean` | No | When true, a model also writes an answer from the retrieved records. The default is false: the response has the records and no answer. |
| `graph_uuids` | `string[]` | Yes | The graph UUIDs to query. The current release accepts one UUID. |
| `guidance` | `string` | No | Optional caller guidance for the answer. The agent treats it as untrusted text. |
| `mode` | `fast \| deep` | No | — |
| `query` | `string` | Yes | The natural-language query for the graph. |
| `stream` | `true` | Yes | When true, return the retrieval calls as server-sent events. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `text` | `string` | Yes | The response body as text. |
| `contentType` | `string \| null` | Yes | The content type Zep answered with. |



---

### searchEdges

`graphs.searchEdges`

Search edges. Edge search does not support node_labels, exclude_node_labels, or content_policy_violated. content_policy_violated is supported on episode search only. An edge has two nodes, so a node label filter is not defined for edge search.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.searchEdges({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', query: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. Search ranks a limited set of results, so iteration ends after that set. To get the top N results, set `page_size` to N and read the first page. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `query` | `string` | Yes | The search query. |
| `bfs_origin_node_uuids` | `string[]` | No | Nodes used as BFS origins for graph-distance-aware retrieval. |
| `center_node_uuid` | `string` | No | The node to rank results by distance from. Required when reranker is node_distance. |
| `filter_low_quality_results` | `boolean` | No | The default is false. When true, Zep removes low-quality results at the knee of the cross-encoder score curve. The filter never removes a result with a score of 0.9 or more. It keeps at least 5 results, or all results when fewer than 5 are available. The filter only removes results, so a page still returns up to page_size results. Use this field only with reranker cross_encoder. It is not supported for episode search. Invalid combinations return a 400 error. The cutoff is approximate, not guaranteed. When the cross-encoder does not score the results, for example after a timeout, Zep returns the results without the filter. |
| `filters` | `object` | No | — |
| `mmr_lambda` | `number` | No | The diversity weighting used for maximal marginal relevance reranking. Required when reranker is mmr. |
| `reranker` | `rrf \| mmr \| node_distance \| episode_mentions \| cross_encoder` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="filters full type">

```ts
{
  connected_node_uuids?: string[],
  date_filters?: {
    any_of?: {
      all_of?: {
        field?: created_at | valid_at | invalid_at | expired_at,
        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | is_null | is_not_null,
        value?: string
      }[]
    }[]
  },

  edge_uuids?: string[],

    filters?: {
      operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
      property_name?: string,
      value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
    }[],
    groups?: {
      filters?: {
        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
        property_name?: string,
        value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
      }[],
      groups?: (recursive)[],
      type?: and | or
    }[],
    type?: and | or
  },

    operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | is_null | is_not_null,
    property_name?: string,
    value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
  }[],
  source_node_uuids?: string[],
  target_node_uuids?: string[]
}
```

### searchEpisodes

`graphs.searchEpisodes`

Search episodes. Episode search supports only the metadata_filters and content_policy_violated filters. Every other filter returns unsupported_filter.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.searchEpisodes({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', query: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. Search ranks a limited set of results, so iteration ends after that set. To get the top N results, set `page_size` to N and read the first page. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `query` | `string` | Yes | The search query. |
| `bfs_origin_node_uuids` | `string[]` | No | Nodes used as BFS origins for graph-distance-aware retrieval. |
| `center_node_uuid` | `string` | No | The node to rank results by distance from. Required when reranker is node_distance. |
| `filter_low_quality_results` | `boolean` | No | The default is false. When true, Zep removes low-quality results at the knee of the cross-encoder score curve. The filter never removes a result with a score of 0.9 or more. It keeps at least 5 results, or all results when fewer than 5 are available. The filter only removes results, so a page still returns up to page_size results. Use this field only with reranker cross_encoder. It is not supported for episode search. Invalid combinations return a 400 error. The cutoff is approximate, not guaranteed. When the cross-encoder does not score the results, for example after a timeout, Zep returns the results without the filter. |
| `filters` | `object` | No | — |
| `mmr_lambda` | `number` | No | The diversity weighting used for maximal marginal relevance reranking. Required when reranker is mmr. |
| `reranker` | `rrf \| mmr \| node_distance \| episode_mentions \| cross_encoder` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="filters full type">

```ts
{
  content_policy_violated?: boolean,
  metadata_filters?: {
    filters?: {
      operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
      property_name?: string,
      value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
    }[],
    groups?: {
      filters?: {
        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
        property_name?: string,
        value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
      }[],
      groups?: (recursive)[],
      type?: and | or
    }[],
    type?: and | or
  }
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The episodes on this page. |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | Opaque cursor for retrieving the next page, present only when more results are available. |
| `total_size` | `number \| null` | No | Omitted for this collection; use next_cursor to detect the end of pagination. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  content?: string | null,
  content_policy?: {
    category_keys?: string[] | null,
    dropped_count?: number | null,
    retained_count?: number | null,
    revision?: number | null,
    status?: string | null,
    violated?: boolean | null
  } | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  document_id?: string | null,
  expired_at?: string | null,
  graph_uuid?: string | null,
  metadata?: Record<string, any> | null,
  processed?: boolean | null,
  reference_time?: string | null,
  relevance?: number | null,
  role?: string | null,
  role_name?: string | null,
  score?: number | null,
  source?: string | null,
  source_description?: string | null,
  thread_uuid?: string | null,
  user_uuid?: string | null,
  uuid?: string | null,
  wait_status?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### searchNodes

`graphs.searchNodes`

Search nodes. Node search does not support edge_types, exclude_edge_types, edge_uuids, connected_node_uuids, source_node_uuids, target_node_uuids, date_filters, or content_policy_violated. content_policy_violated is supported on episode search only. The edge filters select edges.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.searchNodes({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c', query: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. Search ranks a limited set of results, so iteration ends after that set. To get the top N results, set `page_size` to N and read the first page. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `query` | `string` | Yes | The search query. |
| `bfs_origin_node_uuids` | `string[]` | No | Nodes used as BFS origins for graph-distance-aware retrieval. |
| `center_node_uuid` | `string` | No | The node to rank results by distance from. Required when reranker is node_distance. |
| `filter_low_quality_results` | `boolean` | No | The default is false. When true, Zep removes low-quality results at the knee of the cross-encoder score curve. The filter never removes a result with a score of 0.9 or more. It keeps at least 5 results, or all results when fewer than 5 are available. The filter only removes results, so a page still returns up to page_size results. Use this field only with reranker cross_encoder. It is not supported for episode search. Invalid combinations return a 400 error. The cutoff is approximate, not guaranteed. When the cross-encoder does not score the results, for example after a timeout, Zep returns the results without the filter. |
| `filters` | `object` | No | — |
| `mmr_lambda` | `number` | No | The diversity weighting used for maximal marginal relevance reranking. Required when reranker is mmr. |
| `reranker` | `rrf \| mmr \| node_distance \| episode_mentions \| cross_encoder` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="filters full type">

```ts
{
  episode_uuids?: string[],
  exclude_node_labels?: string[],
  metadata_filters?: {
    filters?: {
      operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
      property_name?: string,
      value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
    }[],
    groups?: {
      filters?: {
        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
        property_name?: string,
        value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
      }[],
      groups?: (recursive)[],
      type?: and | or
    }[],
    type?: and | or
  },
  node_labels?: string[],
  property_filters?: {
    operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | is_null | is_not_null,
    property_name?: string,
    value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
  }[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The nodes on this page. |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | The cursor to pass as the next request's cursor to fetch the following page; absent when no further pages remain. |
| `total_size` | `number \| null` | No | Omitted for this collection; use next_cursor to detect the end of pagination. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  attributes?: Record<string, any> | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  degree?: number | null,
  episode_uuids?: string[] | null,
  episode_uuids_truncated?: boolean | null,
  graph_uuid?: string | null,
  labels?: string[] | null,
  name?: string | null,
  relevance?: number | null,
  score?: number | null,
  summary?: string | null,
  user_uuid?: string | null,
  uuid?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

### setInclusionRules

`graphs.setInclusionRules`

Set graph inclusion rules. Replaces the graph rules. Request: &#123;"inclusion_rules":[]&#125;. Response: &#123;"inclusion_rules":[],"inherited":true&#125;. A free project answered 404 not_found to both the read and the write (11-27): the feature may need enabling on the account.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.setInclusionRules({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Graph UUID |
| `inclusion_rules` | `object[]` | No | InclusionRules replaces the complete rule set at this scope. |
| `inherited` | `boolean` | No | Inherited identifies a graph that uses project defaults. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="inclusion_rules full type">

```ts
{
  condition?: string,
  effect?: include | exclude,
  name?: string,
  target?: entity | fact,
  types?: string[]
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `inclusion_rules` | `object[] \| null` | No | InclusionRules replaces the complete rule set at this scope. |
| `inherited` | `boolean \| null` | No | Inherited identifies a graph that uses project defaults. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="inclusion_rules full type">

```ts
{
  condition?: string | null,
  effect?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  target?: string | null,
  types?: string[] | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### threadSummaries.list

`graphs.threadSummaries.list`

List thread summaries. Lists the thread summaries of a graph and returns one page of results. The default page_size is 50, and the maximum page_size is 100. You can sort by uuid or created_at. The default sort is uuid in descending order.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.graphs.threadSummaries.list({ graph_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `graph_uuid` | `string` | Yes | The UUID of the graph. For a user graph, you can also use the UUID of the user. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `order_by` | `uuid \| created_at` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `filters` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="filters full type">

```ts
{
  date_filters?: {
    any_of?: {
      all_of?: {
        field?: created_at | valid_at | invalid_at | expired_at,
        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | is_null | is_not_null,
        value?: string
      }[]
    }[]
  },
  metadata_filters?: {
    filters?: {
      operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
      property_name?: string,
      value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
    }[],
    groups?: {
      filters?: {
        operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | contains | is_null | is_not_null,
        property_name?: string,
        value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
      }[],
      groups?: (recursive)[],
      type?: and | or
    }[],
    type?: and | or
  },
  property_filters?: {
    operator?: eq | ne | gt | lt | gte | lte | in | is_null | is_not_null,
    property_name?: string,
    value?: string | number | boolean | (string | number | boolean)[]
  }[]
}
```

| `inherited` | `boolean \| null` | No | Inherited identifies a graph that uses project defaults. |

### update

`project.update`

Update project. Set hyperedges_enabled to true to enable extraction. A member needs the project.edit permission. Project API keys receive 403 permission_denied.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.project.update({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `default_time_zone` | `string` | No | The project's IANA fallback time zone. Set to null to clear the existing value. |
| `hyperedges_enabled` | `boolean` | No | Set to true to enable hyperedge extraction. This setting cannot be disabled. A member needs project.edit. Project API keys receive 403 permission_denied. |
| `include_policy_violating_episodes` | `boolean` | No | When true, episode reads on graphs with a content policy include the episodes that violated the policy. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the project was created. |
| `default_time_zone` | `string \| null` | No | The project's default IANA time zone, used as the fallback when a resource does not set its own. |
| `description` | `string \| null` | No | A human-readable description of the project. |
| `hyperedges_enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | True when hyperedge extraction is enabled for this project. This setting is one-way. |
| `include_policy_violating_episodes` | `boolean \| null` | No | When true, episode reads on graphs with a content policy include the episodes that violated the policy. The default is false. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the project. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the project. |

`tasks.get`

**Output**

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `completed_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the task reached a terminal status; absent while it is still in progress. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the task was created. |
| `error` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `progress` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `result` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `started_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time a worker began processing the task; absent until then. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | The service returns the task state. Valid values are pending, processing, succeeded, partial, and failed. A task has no canceled state. When a batch becomes canceled, its batch.process task becomes failed, and the task error code is batch_canceled. |
| `type` | `string \| null` | No | The kind of asynchronous operation the task represents. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the task was last updated. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the task, used to poll its status. |

### messages.add

`threads.messages.add`

Add thread messages. (POST /threads/&#123;thread_uuid&#125;/messages)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
	thread_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c',
	messages: [{ role: 'user', name: 'Ada', content: 'I moved to Lisbon last month.' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'Noted, Lisbon it is.' }],
});
```

| `messages` | `object[]` | Yes | The messages to add to the thread. |
| `return_context` | `boolean` | No | When true, returns the context block for the thread's most recent messages. |
| `strict_ontology` | `boolean` | No | When true, prevents extraction of generic entity nodes that do not match the configured ontology. |

## Trace Connections

### create

`traceConnections.create`

Create a trace connection. Verify a provider credential before Zep stores it. Both providers accept 1 to 60 requests per minute and an optional retention window.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.traceConnections.create({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `api_url` | `string` | No | APIURL is an optional custom provider endpoint. |
| `credential` | `string` | No | Credential is the write-only provider credential. |
| `name` | `string` | No | Name is the connection name. |
| `provider` | `braintrust \| langsmith` | No | Provider is the Braintrust or LangSmith provider. |
| `requests_per_minute` | `number` | No | RequestsPerMinute is the request rate limit from 1 to 60 per minute. |
| `retention_days` | `number` | No | RetentionDays is an optional retention window in days. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | CreatedAt is the resource creation time. |
| `credential_hint` | `string \| null` | No | CredentialHint contains the final four characters of the credential. |
| `credential_version` | `number \| null` | No | CredentialVersion is the current credential version. |
| `import_count` | `number \| null` | No | ImportCount is the number of non-deleted imports that use this connection. |
| `last_verified_at` | `string \| null` | No | LastVerifiedAt is the last successful credential verification time. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | Name is the display name. |
| `provider` | `string \| null` | No | Provider is the Braintrust or LangSmith provider. |
| `provider_organization_id` | `string \| null` | No | ProviderOrganizationID is the provider organization identifier. |
| `provider_organization_name` | `string \| null` | No | ProviderOrganizationName is the provider organization name. |
| `settings` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | Status is the provider verification status. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | UpdatedAt is the last resource update time. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | UUID is the trace connection identifier. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="settings full type">

```ts
{
  api_url?: string | null,
  requests_per_minute?: number | null,
  retention_days?: number | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### getTrace

`traceConnections.getTrace`

**Risk:** `read`

In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `filter` | `object` | No | — |
| `provider_project_id` | `string` | Yes | The developer supplies the project ID from the selected provider. |

### update

`traceConnections.update`

Update a trace connection. Verify changed settings before Zep stores them. A request that changes api_url must also include credential. Otherwise Zep returns 400 invalid_request. Both providers accept 1 to 60 requests per minute and an optional retention window. Example request: &#123;"requests_per_minute":60,"retention_days":30&#125;.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.traceConnections.update({ connection_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `connection_uuid` | `string` | Yes | Trace connection UUID |
| `api_url` | `string` | No | APIURL is the new custom provider endpoint, when supplied. A request that changes api_url must also include credential. |
| `credential` | `string` | No | Credential is the new write-only provider credential, when supplied. |
| `name` | `string` | No | Name is the new connection name, when supplied. |
| `requests_per_minute` | `number` | No | RequestsPerMinute is the new request rate limit, when supplied. |
| `retention_days` | `number` | No | RetentionDays is the new retention window in days, when supplied. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | CreatedAt is the resource creation time. |
| `credential_hint` | `string \| null` | No | CredentialHint contains the final four characters of the credential. |
| `credential_version` | `number \| null` | No | CredentialVersion is the current credential version. |
| `import_count` | `number \| null` | No | ImportCount is the number of non-deleted imports that use this connection. |
| `last_verified_at` | `string \| null` | No | LastVerifiedAt is the last successful credential verification time. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | Name is the display name. |
| `provider` | `string \| null` | No | Provider is the Braintrust or LangSmith provider. |
| `provider_organization_id` | `string \| null` | No | ProviderOrganizationID is the provider organization identifier. |
| `provider_organization_name` | `string \| null` | No | ProviderOrganizationName is the provider organization name. |
| `settings` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | Status is the provider verification status. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | UpdatedAt is the last resource update time. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | UUID is the trace connection identifier. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="settings full type">

```ts
{
  api_url?: string | null,
  requests_per_minute?: number | null,
  retention_days?: number | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Users

### create

`users.create`

Create user. (POST /users)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.users.create({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `content_policy` | `object` | No | — |
| `disable_default_ontology` | `boolean` | No | When true, disables the default ontology for the user's graph. |
| `email` | `string` | No | The email address of the user. |
| `first_name` | `string` | No | The user's first name. |
| `last_name` | `string` | No | The user's last name. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any>` | No | Metadata to store on the user. |
| `time_zone` | `string` | No | The user's IANA time zone. |
| `user_id` | `string` | No | An optional external identity identifier, such as an identity-provider subject claim. Zep uses it to match an identity-provider sign-in to this user. It is not an address; use uuid to address the user in v4. It is unique within the project, can contain at most 500 characters, and cannot be changed after creation. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="content_policy full type">

```ts
{
  categories?: {
    description?: string,
    key?: string
  }[],
  project_revision?: number,
  rules?: {
    action?: exclude | include,
    category_key?: string,
    condition?: string,
    description?: string,
    edge_types?: string[],
    entity_types?: string[],
    patterns?: string[],
    permitted_examples?: string[],
    prohibited_examples?: string[]
  }[]
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the user was created. |
| `disable_default_ontology` | `boolean \| null` | No | When true, disables the use of the default fallback ontology for the user's graph. |
| `email` | `string \| null` | No | The email address of the user. |
| `first_name` | `string \| null` | No | The first name of the user. |
| `graph_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the user's graph, populated when the user is created. |

### getByEmail

`users.getByEmail`

Get user by email. Send &#123;"email": "jane@example.com"&#125; to get the user with this email. An email matches only when the stored and requested values are equal after normalization. Normalization lowercases both values and trims leading and trailing whitespace.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.users.getByEmail({ email: 'ada@example.com' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `email` | `string` | Yes | The user's email address. Matching ignores case and leading and trailing whitespace. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the user was created. |
| `disable_default_ontology` | `boolean \| null` | No | When true, disables the use of the default fallback ontology for the user's graph. |
| `email` | `string \| null` | No | The email address of the user. |
| `first_name` | `string \| null` | No | The first name of the user. |
| `graph_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the user's graph, populated when the user is created. |
| `last_name` | `string \| null` | No | The last name of the user. |
| `metadata` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Arbitrary key-value metadata attached to the user. |
| `time_zone` | `string \| null` | No | The user's IANA time zone, used to localize dates in generated context. |
| `user_id` | `string \| null` | No | An optional external identity identifier, such as an identity-provider subject claim. Zep uses it to match an identity-provider sign-in to this user. It is not an address; use uuid to address the user in v4. It is unique within the project, can contain at most 500 characters, and cannot be changed after creation. v4 sets it on create; v3 users keep their existing value. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the user. |

### getNode

`users.getNode`

Get user node. (GET /users/&#123;user_uuid&#125;/node)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.users.getNode({ user_uuid: '3f0e6a2c-8b1d-4c7e-9a52-1d2e3f4a5b6c' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `user_uuid` | `string` | Yes | User UUID |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `attributes` | `Record<string, any> \| null` | No | Additional attributes of the node, as defined by its entity type. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | The time the node was created. |
| `degree` | `number \| null` | No | The count of live entity edges that touch this node, in both directions. Present only when the list request orders by `degree`. |
| `episode_uuids` | `string[] \| null` | No | The UUIDs of the live episodes that mention this node, newest first. The list is complete when `episode_uuids_truncated` is false. The list holds the newest 100 when the node has more than 100 source episodes; list episodes with the `mentioned_node_uuids` filter to read them all. |
| `episode_uuids_truncated` | `boolean \| null` | No | True when the node has more than 100 source episodes, so `episode_uuids` holds only the newest 100, or when provenance is unavailable. False means `episode_uuids` is the complete set. |
| `graph_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the graph this node belongs to. |
| `labels` | `string[] \| null` | No | The entity type labels assigned to the node. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | The name of the entity that the node represents. |
| `relevance` | `number \| null` | No | A cross-encoder-derived relevance score, present only when the cross-encoder reranker scored this result; like score, it is omitted from plain list results. |
| `score` | `number \| null` | No | The ranking score returned with every search result; omitted when the node appears in a plain list rather than a search result. |
| `summary` | `string \| null` | No | A generated summary of the entity that the node represents. |
| `user_uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The unique identifier of the user that owns the graph. Present only when the graph is a user graph. |
| `uuid` | `string \| null` | No | The node UUID, assigned by Zep. |

### list

`users.list`

List users. (POST /users/list)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.zep.api.users.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page_size` | `number` | No | The maximum number of items in one page. The SDK method returns a pager. In Python, `.items` holds only the first page. In TypeScript, `.data` holds only the first page. To read every page, iterate the pager: use `for` or `list(...)` in Python, or `for await` in TypeScript. In Go, the method returns one page, and `Iterator()` reads all pages. |
| `cursor` | `string` | No | Opaque page cursor |
| `order_by` | `created_at \| user_id \| email \| uuid` | No | — |
| `order` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |
| `search` | `string` | No | Filters results to users whose user ID, email, or name contains this text. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The users on this page. |
| `next_cursor` | `string \| null` | No | The cursor to pass as the next request's cursor to fetch the following page; absent when no further pages remain. |
| `total_size` | `number \| null` | No | The total number of matching users, counted at the time of this response. It does not indicate whether more pages remain. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  created_at?: string | null,
  disable_default_ontology?: boolean | null,
  email?: string | null,
  first_name?: string | null,
  graph_uuid?: string | null,
  last_name?: string | null,
  metadata?: Record<string, any> | null,
  time_zone?: string | null,
  user_id?: string | null,
  uuid?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>
